  Recommended task executor category: deep-low - Resolve the complete requirement, dependency and ownership graph against evidence.
  Exact command: `cargo run --locked --manifest-path probes/Cargo.toml --bin plan-audit -- --plan .omo/plans/ovis-terminal-v1.md --ideals docs/design/ideal-state.md --gaps docs/design/gap-list.md --scope-root . --deny-write-root /home/devswha/workspace/ovis --require-todos 1-70 --require-ideals 117 --require-gaps 150 --require-exclusions 27 --reject-unresolved --evidence "$ATTEMPT_DIR/F1-ovis-terminal-v1.json"` exits 0 with uncovered_ideals:[], uncovered_gaps:[], excluded_features:[], unresolved_refs:[], write_conflicts:[]. Review every borrowed design against THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md and reject an Ovis edit or an excluded implementation even if a checker misses it.

- [ ] F2. Code quality review (clippy -D warnings, pure-LOC/param checker, violations.md rules as a checklist)
  Recommended task executor category: deep-low - Audit semantic rules as well as mechanical gates without weakening failures.
  Exact command: `cargo fmt --all -- --check && cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings && cargo nextest run --workspace --all-targets --all-features && cargo test --workspace --doc --all-features && cargo deny check && cargo run --locked --manifest-path probes/Cargo.toml --bin source-check -- --roots crates packages/client-ts conformance bench probes --max-pure-loc 250 --max-params 3 --violations docs/research/violations.md --checklist docs/quality-checklist.md --evidence "$ATTEMPT_DIR/F2-ovis-terminal-v1.json"` exits 0. For unavoidable unsafe wrappers also run `cargo +nightly miri nextest run -p ovis-terminal-holder --no-default-features --features ffi-model` and attach native Windows FFI test receipts; Miri only proves the portable wrapper model. Apply every Rule totals identifier in violations.md as a checked review item, with source rule precedence, V-row examples, evidence and a reason for any N/A; no ignored warning, production panic/unwrap, dropped error, unchecked cast, oversized module, blanket allow, detached task, lock-across-await, fixed-sleep test or secret log is acceptable. Generated bulk files must live only in build output, not be exempted by name.

- [ ] F3. Real manual QA (conformance runner against a freshly built binary in an isolated session: every scenario family)
  Recommended task executor category: deep-low - Inspect real binary transcripts across all domain families on Linux and macOS.
  Exact command: `cargo build --locked -p ovis-terminal-cli --bin ovis-terminal && cargo run --locked --manifest-path conformance/Cargo.toml --bin conformance -- --binary target/debug/ovis-terminal --scenario all/all --case all --isolated --require-no-skips --manual-transcript --evidence "$ATTEMPT_DIR/F3-ovis-terminal-v1.json"` exits 0 with failed:0 and skipped:0 for protocol, events, lifecycle, layout, attach, input, waits, agent, remote, services, recovery, security, observability, CLI, client, release and performance smoke families. Execute separately on ubuntu and macos; platform-specific Windows cases are proven by F5, not silently counted as Unix skips. Inspect request/result pairs, terminal byte captures, actual process/start tokens, files and cleanup inventory; an expected typed error is a passing negative case, not a missing scenario. Long-duration performance is separately required by todos 69/70 and F6.

- [ ] F5. Windows CI smoke green (named pipe hello + ConPTY run/read/kill)

- [ ] F6. Performance budget (50-terminal bench p99 within IDEAL latency rows)
  Recommended task executor category: quick - Execute the fixed workload and read its unfiltered latency distributions.
  Exact command: `cargo build --locked --release -p ovis-terminal-cli --bin ovis-terminal && cargo run --locked --release --manifest-path bench/Cargo.toml --bin terminal50 -- --binary target/release/ovis-terminal --isolated --terminals 50 --clients 8 --inflight 16 --output-bytes-per-second 1048576 --samples 10000 --admission-p99-ms 100 --event-p99-ms 100 --evidence "$ATTEMPT_DIR/F6-ovis-terminal-v1.json"` exits 0 with admission_p99_ms<=100, event_p99_ms<=100, no healthy-viewer stall and no unbounded queues; record host, build, warmup, full histogram and overload counts. Also execute `cargo run --locked --release --manifest-path bench/Cargo.toml --bin terminal50 -- --binary target/release/ovis-terminal --isolated --terminals 16 --clients 8 --inflight 16 --output-bytes-per-second 1048576 --samples 10000 --admission-max-ms 100 --event-max-ms 100 --evidence "$ATTEMPT_DIR/F6-ideal-profile.json"` and require every admitted sample <=100 ms; the 50-terminal p99 is a separately reported capacity gate, not the IDEAL service profile. For implementation completion retain the 16-terminal binding-profile result from Todo 69, Todo 70's continuous 30-minute receipt and the scheduled 72-hour dispatch references. For release completion require both native 72-hour end receipts from Todo 70; neither an average nor the shorter implementation proof substitutes for them.

## Commit strategy

Use one PR per numbered todo lane on `devswha/ovis-terminal`, on branch `plan/ovis-terminal-v1-task-N` from its landed dependencies. Each lane includes implementation, unit/property tests, real-surface conformance, fixtures and the declared evidence; it must be independently buildable and green. Parallel lanes have disjoint source ownership within a wave, including protocol/dispatch leaf files, and no worker edits shared Cargo manifests, crate entry points, generated released schema or another lane's paths. The scaffold supplies the declared module/route slots; Todo 1 owns shared seams before dispatch; leaf owners edit existing files and do not wait for dependent assembly to compile their crate. Every Blocked by edge points to a strictly earlier topological wave. A dependency's downstream Blocks list includes every consumer and final verification. Implementation complete requires all todos 1-70 plus F1-F6 with the continuous 30-minute soak proof; release complete additionally requires the scheduled 72-hour Linux/macOS results and rerun release audits.

Write Conventional Commits in English using each todo's `Commit:` subject. Keep a verified increment atomic; no unrelated refactor, test weakening or generated-output churn. The orchestrator alone squash-merges after review and required CI; workers do not merge, force-push, publish packages, create release tags or modify the read-only Ovis repository. Package publication and release activation are explicit orchestrator steps only after release completion (F1-F6 plus scheduled 72-hour results), not side effects of a lane's dry-run.

Every PR body MUST contain literal `## Before` and `## After` sections. Before pastes a cited herdr/hc transcript line or the literal word `absent` for a capability with no comparable baseline, clearly distinguishing live observation from a new-product absence. After pastes exact command, stdout/stderr channel summary, numeric exit code, typed receipts, identity/sequence assertions and negative-case output; a link or a screenshot alone is insufficient. Include the binary/schema/build SHA, platform, test counts, resource cleanup and no-Ovis-write proof, plus before/after metrics for performance changes. Explain any shortfall rather than marking a todo complete.

Evidence lives under `<attemptDir>/task-N-ovis-terminal-v1.json` with raw companions `task-N-ovis-terminal-v1.stdout.log`, `.stderr.log`, `.wire.jsonl`, `.bytes.bin` when applicable; `<attemptDir>` is the absolute `ATTEMPT_DIR` created for this execution. Todo 1 copies redacted baseline transcripts into `docs/evidence/baseline/` with original path/line and hash. Keep the Before excerpts' original paths under `/tmp/ulw-ovis-yCJzPH/evidence/smoke/`, and retain source hashes when copying into an artifact. Upload these as PR/CI artifacts without credentials or secret-input payloads, index them in `<attemptDir>/manifest.json`, and include stable artifact/run URLs and hashes in the PR body. Final evidence uses `F1-ovis-terminal-v1.json` through `F6-ovis-terminal-v1.json`; OS-scoped subdirectories prevent one platform overwriting another. The 72-hour harness checkpoints to artifact storage while running, then publishes a signed/hash-checked end receipt with actual continuous elapsed time. No evidence generated during this drafting task claims implementation exists.
